constraints are exactly **three** (PK, the `kind` CHECK, the `auth.users` FK `on delete cascade`) with **no FK to any content table**; `record_study_event` exists with `prosecdef = true` and `proconfig search_path=public`. **P5.2** added `src/lib/study-events.ts` (the single write path — RPC only, **fails open, loudly**) and wired the four emitters on their **success paths only**: `quiz_submitted` (gated on **≥1 in-range attempted answer**, sharing the hoisted **`isAttempted`** predicate with the answer reveal, so the reveal and the streak can never drift), `summary_generated` (after persist; **NOT** on the cached branch — re-reading a stored summary is `summary_read`, deliberately excluded), `question_asked` (**inside the stream closure**, after the `for await` drains — the route returns a **200 stream before Claude speaks**, and the conversation-limit denial **also returns 200**, so no return-site gate is correct), and `material_uploaded` (after `status='ready'`; ingest is **fully synchronous**, so it cannot succeed while still processing). **Live-verified:** an **empty quiz submission emitted NOTHING** (zero rows) and a real attempt emitted **exactly one** `quiz_submitted` row with a real `occurred_at`; a **cached summary re-open emitted NOTHING**. **`summary_generated` and `question_asked` were NOT exercised** (needed an unsummarized doc / a paid Claude call) — treat as **unverified code**. **Two risks merged unfixed:** the agent emit fires **after headers are flushed**, so a serverless teardown can lose a `question_asked` event (**event multiplicity is the mitigation, not a fix**); and ingest's final `documents` update has a **pre-existing unchecked error**, so a failed status write leaves the row `processing` while the route returns `success:true` **and emits** — the one path where "emitted" and "actually ready" disagree. **P5.3** adds `20260710_current_streak.sql` — **`current_streak(text)`, SECURITY INVOKER** (the read needs no elevated power; RLS scopes it), `stable`, `search_path=public`, which buckets `occurred_at` via **`at time zone`** into days in the **student's** zone and walks the `(user_id, occurred_at desc)` index **newest-first, exiting at the first gap**. The zone is an **IANA NAME, never a numeric offset** (`Africa/Casablanca` reverts to **UTC+0 for Ramadan** — a stored offset misfiles the month students study hardest), supplied per request by the browser, **validated against `pg_timezone_names`** and **falling back to `Africa/Casablanca`** rather than raising at the student. **`profiles.timezone` was REJECTED on evidence:** `001_initial_schema.sql:11` is `create policy "Users can manage own profile" on profiles for all using (auth.uid() = id)` — a single **`for all`** policy — and signup already writes `profiles` **from the browser** with the anon key, so a column there is **just as client-writable** as a parameter, buys **zero** forgery resistance, and adds a **staleness bug** that lies **per-user, silently, permanently** (Casablanca → Dubai, never updated). A **fixed zone was also rejected**: Casablanca-midnight is **03:00 in Dubai**, so it misfiles a Gulf student's post-midnight sessions across a **3-hour** window — **three times worse** than the 1-hour plain-UTC bug that INVARIANT 2 was written to kill. **Grace decision:** the newest study day must be **today or yesterday**; if yesterday, the streak **survives until today ends** and shows the run **through yesterday** (every day of it earned). A streak that snaps at **00:01** punishes a student for sleeping; a streak that counts today before it is earned promises what the app has not measured. Neither claim is made. `null` (**not measured**) stays distinct from `0` (**a real, earned zero**) end to end — every read failure and the pre-cookie first paint yield `null`, so the P2.2 **ghost placeholder** renders, per §5's "Honest placeholders". `dashboard/page.tsx` **stays a server component**: a tiny `<TimeZoneSync/>` client component writes a `kf_tz` cookie and calls `router.refresh()` once, and the page reads it with the **`next/headers` machinery `createClient()` already uses**. The streak card now states the zone it counts in (`streakZoneHint` — "in your local time" / "بتوقيتك المحلّي"), **suppressed beside the ghost** (a hint about a number that does not exist is itself a false promise). **Register #36 extended:** the zone is **client-asserted**; a student can re-bucket existing history by claiming another zone, shifting boundaries by **at most ~26h**, and can **never fabricate a day on which nothing happened** (two events >48h apart are non-adjacent under **every** zone on Earth). Self-cheating while a streak grants nothing — **same Phase-9 trigger** as revoking the RPC grant.

### 2026-07-09 - Phase 4 (Quizzes) DONE: P4.3 quiz-taking UI (PR #32)

**Phase 4 (Quizzes) — ✅ DONE. P4.3 (quiz-taking UI) MERGED via PR #32, merge `0910e63`** (merge commit, on top of the P4.2 docs merge `7a2a305`), completing **P4.0 → P4.3**. Ships **`QuizSection`** on the document-detail page: the quiz is obtained via **`POST /api/quiz/generate`** (a cached hit is **free** — no Claude call, no charge), and the UI performs **ZERO client-side reads of `quizzes`/`quiz_items`** — which **closes register #29 STRUCTURALLY, not by discipline**: there is no client query of the answer table, so no `select('*')` can leak `correct_index`. Two correctness traps are isolated in **named helpers** (so reintroducing either means deleting a documented function): **`revealedIndex()`** tests **`'correct_index' in r`**, never truthiness — a truthiness check would make a correct answer of **option 0** permanently invisible; **`answeredIndex()`** returns `null` for an out-of-range value, so `options[]` is **never** indexed with a raw echoed `selected_index`. The **error map was written from the route bytes, not copied from `summarize`**: `/api/quiz/generate` can also return **503** (`enforceLimit` **fail-closed** when `increment_usage` errors) and **400**, and **`/api/quiz/submit`'s 409 means "this quiz has no items"** — a **different meaning** from `summarize`'s 409 "still processing" — so they carry **separate strings**. Correct-state styling reuses the **`--primary` tokens** (already `#047857`, emerald-700) rather than a raw `green-700` (`#15803d`), which would have planted a **second, near-but-unequal green** beside the theme primary — the drift **P2.7** purged. **Live-verified on Preview `b8a9198`:** the raw generate response body's **`.includes('correct_index')` returned `false`**; five in-range answers → **`1/5`** with the correct answer revealed under **each wrong item** and **no redundant reveal** under the correct one; **zero selections → every item "not answered" with NO correct answer revealed anywhere** (the empty-submission bypass, proven **end-to-end through the UI**). **Register #29 is now CLOSED** (all three surfaces proven). **"DONE" = the feature shipped — it does NOT close the cross-cutting items Phase 4 surfaced:** **#30** (non-atomic two-insert quiz creation), **#31** (quiz language permanently fixed at generation time), **#32** (submission throttle) and **#33** (`quiz_attempts` is **write-only + best-effort, NOT complete**) all remain **open/deferred**. **Phase 5 MUST read #33 before building any streak on those attempt rows.** **Next up: Phase 5 (Streak & progress).**

### 2026-07-09 - P4.2 quiz grading route (PR #30)

**P4.2 (quiz grading route) MERGED via PR #30, merge `ad2b55d`** (merge commit, on top of the P4.1 docs merge `c53c20f`). Ships **`POST /api/quiz/submit`** — **server-side grading**, a **UUID shape-check BEFORE any DB query** (a malformed id is a **400**, never a Postgres `22P02` surfacing as a **500**), **401** on no user, **RLS-scoped 404** (not-found and not-owned are indistinguishable), a **zero-item guard** (**409**; register **#30**'s orphan-quiz window would otherwise grade `0/0`), a **200-answer cap**, **first-occurrence-wins** on a duplicate `item_id`, **unanswered = incorrect** (never an error), **foreign `item_id`s ignored** (cannot affect the score), **no `enforceLimit`** (grading is not a paid call — charging `'quiz'` would wrongly drain the **generation** cap), a **fail-open `quiz_attempts` insert** (a bookkeeping write must never lose an already-computed grade), and an index on **`(quiz_id, created_at desc)`**. **Feedback model (decided 2026-07-09):** **multiple attempts allowed**, and **`correct_index` is revealed per item ONLY for items attempted with an IN-RANGE `selected_index`** — "attempted" is an in-range choice, **not** merely a non-null one, because a non-null test would let `selected_index: 99` on every item harvest the whole key in one request. Migration `20260709_quiz_attempts.sql` was **applied to the live DB and verified**. **Live-verified on Preview `9f26a53`** (evidence named in register **#29**): an **empty `answers` array → truthful `0/5` with ZERO `correct_index` fields**; **`selected_index: 99` → graded incorrect, no `correct_index` revealed**; **five in-range answers → `3/5`, `correct_index` revealed on all attempted items**; **three `quiz_attempts` rows persisted** (`0/5`, `0/5`, `3/5`); and `/api/quiz/generate` **re-audited byte-identical on the merged tree** (blob `e7248510`) — the key never leaves it. Register **#29** stays **OPEN** on **one remaining count only: P4.3's UI**. Registers **#32** (submission throttle, deferred) and **#33** (`quiz_attempts` is write-only + best-effort, never assume complete) stand as recorded. **Phase 4 is STILL IN PROGRESS — only P4.3 (UI) remains.**

### 2026-07-09 - P4.1 quiz-generation route (PR #28)

**P4.1 (quiz-generation route) MERGED via PR #28, merge `5338ffb`** (merge commit, on top of the P4.0 merge `a76c0be`). Ships **`POST /api/quiz/generate`** — **document-scoped** generation via Haiku (`claude-haiku-4-5-20251001`), a **strict-JSON** prompt with an **assistant prefill** (`"["`), a **defensive parse + all-or-nothing shape validation** (any violation → **502**, **nothing persisted** — never a partial quiz), the **cache-existence check BEFORE `enforceLimit`** (a cached re-read **never charges** a quiz credit), the **thin-content guard before `enforceLimit`** (a no-op request burns no counter), `enforceLimit('quiz')` in front of the paid call, **`is_partial` persisted on generate and read back on cache-hit**, and **`correct_index` server-only** — written once on insert, **never in any read select, never returned**. Migration `20260708_quizzes_is_partial.sql` (`is_partial boolean not null default false`) was **applied to the live DB and verified** via `information_schema` (standing rule §5). **Live-verified on Preview `af2ccbe`** (see register **#29**): 5 items, `is_partial` false, cached re-read `cached:true` with **no Claude call and no counter charge**, `correct_index` **absent from the raw network payload** and **present + correct in the DB** (all 5 verified against their options; `options_count` 4 each, inside the 2–5 CHECK), an **English document produced Arabic questions under `locale:'ar'`**, and a **nonexistent `document_id` returned 404 before `enforceLimit`** (no charge, no existence leak). Register **#29** stays **OPEN** — its generate-route half is now proven, but it is **re-scoped route-invariant** (P4.2 grading + P4.3 UI still unproven). New registers: **#30** (non-atomic two-insert quiz creation, accepted for P4.1) and **#31** (quiz language is fixed at generation time, permanently — the generate-once corollary of #27). **Phase 4 is STILL IN PROGRESS — P4.2 (taking/grading) and P4.3 (UI) remain.**

### 2026-07-08 - P4.0 quizzes schema + types + rate-limit (PR #26)

**P4.0 (schema + types + rate-limit) MERGED via PR #26, merge `a76c0be`** (merge commit, on top of the Phase-3 merge `a05ab40`). Ships the **document-scoped** `quizzes`/`quiz_items` tables with **server-only `correct_index`** (never client-exposed), `usage_counters.quiz_count` + the `increment_usage` `'quiz'` branch, the `Quiz`/`QuizItem`/`ClientQuizItem` types, and the `rate-limit` `'quiz'` kind (free **5** / pro **100**, exhaustive `KIND_NOUN` map). Migration `20260708_quizzes.sql` was **applied to the live DB and verified** (tables, constraints, `quiz_count`, `increment_usage` all confirmed present). P4.0 design decisions recorded as register **#28**; the P4.2 leak-guard requirement as register **#29**. **Phase 4 is IN PROGRESS — P4.1–P4.3 (generation, taking/grading, UI) remain.**
